1→"""Debug and monitoring utilities for travel between images tasks."""
     2→
     3→import os
     4→from pathlib import Path
     5→
     6→# Import structured logging
     7→from ...core.log import travel_logger
     8→
     9→# RAM monitoring
    10→try:
    11→    import psutil
    12→    _PSUTIL_AVAILABLE = True
    13→except ImportError:
    14→    _PSUTIL_AVAILABLE = False
    15→    travel_logger.warning("psutil not available - RAM monitoring disabled")
    16→
    17→try:
    18→    import cv2
    19→    _COLOR_MATCH_DEPS_AVAILABLE = True
    20→except ImportError:
    21→    _COLOR_MATCH_DEPS_AVAILABLE = False
    22→
    23→from ...utils import get_video_frame_count_and_fps
    24→from source.core.constants import BYTES_PER_MB, MB_PER_GB, BYTES_PER_GB
    25→
    26→
    27→# Add debugging helper function
    28→def debug_video_analysis(video_path: str | Path, label: str, task_id: str = "unknown") -> dict:
    29→    """Analyze a video file and return comprehensive debug info"""
    30→    try:
    31→        path_obj = Path(video_path)
    32→        if not path_obj.exists():
    33→            travel_logger.debug(f"{label}: FILE MISSING - {video_path}", task_id=task_id)
    34→            return {"exists": False, "path": str(video_path)}
    35→
    36→        frame_count, fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(path_obj))
    37→        file_size = path_obj.stat().st_size
    38→        duration = frame_count / fps if fps and fps > 0 else 0
    39→
    40→        debug_info = {
    41→            "exists": True,
    42→            "path": str(path_obj.resolve()),
    43→            "frame_count": frame_count,
    44→            "fps": fps,
    45→            "duration_seconds": duration,
    46→            "file_size_bytes": file_size,
    47→            "file_size_mb": round(file_size / BYTES_PER_MB, 2)
    48→        }
    49→
    50→        travel_logger.debug(f"{label}: {debug_info['frame_count']} frames, {debug_info['fps']} fps, {debug_info['duration_seconds']:.2f}s, {debug_info['file_size_mb']} MB", task_id=task_id)
    51→
    52→        # Lightweight color diagnostic: sample a few frames and compute mean BGR.
    53→        # Helps detect "brown tint" / channel or range issues without decoding entire video.
    54→        try:
    55→            if frame_count and frame_count > 0:
    56→                cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path_obj))
    57→                if cap.isOpened():
    58→                    sample_idxs = [0, max(0, int(frame_count // 2)), max(0, int(frame_count - 1))]
    59→                    samples = {}
    60→                    for idx in sample_idxs:
    61→                        cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, float(idx))
    62→                        ok, fr = cap.read()
    63→                        if not ok or fr is None:
    64→                            samples[str(idx)] = None
    65→                            continue
    66→                        # OpenCV frames are BGR uint8; mean per channel is robust for tint detection.
    67→                        mean_bgr = tuple(float(x) for x in fr.mean(axis=(0, 1)))
    68→                        samples[str(idx)] = {
    69→                            "mean_bgr": mean_bgr,
    70→                            "shape": tuple(int(x) for x in fr.shape),
    71→                        }
    72→                    cap.release()
    73→                    debug_info["frame_color_samples"] = samples
    74→                    travel_logger.debug(f"[FrameBrowningIssue] {label}: frame_color_samples={samples}", task_id=task_id)
    75→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_color:
    76→            # Never fail the pipeline due to debug sampling.
    77→            travel_logger.debug(f"[FrameBrowningIssue] {label}: color sample failed: {e_color}", task_id=task_id)
    78→
    79→        return debug_info
    80→
    81→    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
    82→        travel_logger.debug(f"{label}: ERROR analyzing video - {e}", task_id=task_id)
    83→        return {"exists": False, "error": str(e), "path": str(video_path)}
    84→
    85→# RAM monitoring helper function
    86→def log_ram_usage(label: str, task_id: str = "unknown", logger=None) -> dict:
    87→    """
    88→    Log current RAM usage with a descriptive label.
    89→    Returns dict with RAM metrics for programmatic use.
    90→    """
    91→    if logger is None:
    92→        logger = travel_logger
    93→
    94→    if not _PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
    95→        return {"available": False}
    96→
    97→    try:
    98→        process = psutil.Process(os.getpid())
    99→        mem_info = process.memory_info()
   100→        rss_mb = mem_info.rss / BYTES_PER_MB
   101→        rss_gb = rss_mb / MB_PER_GB
   102→
   103→        # Get system-wide memory stats
   104→        sys_mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
   105→        sys_total_gb = sys_mem.total / BYTES_PER_GB
   106→        sys_available_gb = sys_mem.available / BYTES_PER_GB
   107→        sys_used_percent = sys_mem.percent
   108→
   109→        logger.info(
   110→            f"[RAM] {label}: Process={rss_mb:.0f}MB ({rss_gb:.2f}GB) | "
   111→            f"System={sys_used_percent:.1f}% used, {sys_available_gb:.1f}GB/{sys_total_gb:.1f}GB available",
   112→            task_id=task_id
   113→        )
   114→
   115→        return {
   116→            "available": True,
   117→            "process_rss_mb": rss_mb,
   118→            "process_rss_gb": rss_gb,
   119→            "system_total_gb": sys_total_gb,
   120→            "system_available_gb": sys_available_gb,
   121→            "system_used_percent": sys_used_percent
   122→        }
   123→
   124→    except (ProcessLookupError, OSError) as e:
   125→        logger.warning(f"[RAM] Failed to get RAM usage: {e}", task_id=task_id)
   126→        return {"available": False, "error": str(e)}
   127→

    59→@dataclass

   101→_MISSING = object()
   102→
   103→def _get_param(key, *sources, default=_MISSING, prefer_truthy: bool = False):
   104→    """
   105→    Get a parameter from multiple sources with precedence (first source wins).
   106→
   107→    By default, only skips None values.
   108→    If prefer_truthy=True, also skips falsy non-bool values (e.g. "", {}, [], 0),
   109→    matching historical `a or b` fallback semantics while still preserving explicit booleans.
   110→    """
   111→    for source in sources:
   112→        if not source or key not in source:
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        value = source[key]
   116→        if value is None:
   117→            continue
   118→
   119→        if prefer_truthy and not isinstance(value, bool) and not value:
   120→            continue
   121→
   122→        return value
   123→
   124→    return None if default is _MISSING else default
   125→
   126→def _resolve_segment_context(task_params_dict: dict, is_standalone: bool, task_id: str) -> SegmentContext:
   127→    """Resolve mode, orchestrator_details, individual_params, and segment identity.
   128→
   129→    Handles orchestrator vs standalone mode, fetching orchestrator_details from the DB
   130→    if not provided inline.
   131→
   132→    Raises ValueError if required fields are missing.
   133→    """
   134→    segment_params = task_params_dict
   135→    orchestrator_task_id_ref = segment_params.get("orchestrator_task_id_ref")
   136→    orchestrator_run_id = segment_params.get("orchestrator_run_id")
   137→    segment_idx = segment_params.get("segment_index")
   138→
   139→    # Get orchestrator_details (canonical name) or full_orchestrator_payload (legacy alias)
   140→    orchestrator_details: dict = segment_params.get("orchestrator_details") or segment_params.get("full_orchestrator_payload")
   141→
   142→    if is_standalone:
   143→        # Standalone mode: use provided payload, default segment_idx to 0
   144→        if segment_idx is None:
   145→            segment_idx = 0
   146→        if not orchestrator_details:
   147→            raise ValueError(f"Individual travel segment {task_id} missing orchestrator_details")
   148→        headless_logger.debug(f"Running in standalone mode (individual_travel_segment)", task_id=task_id)
   149→    else:
   150→        # Orchestrator mode: require segment_index and either inline orchestrator_details or ability to fetch
   151→        if segment_idx is None:
   152→            raise ValueError(f"Travel segment {task_id} missing segment_index")
   153→
   154→        # If orchestrator_details not inline, try to fetch from parent task
   155→        if not orchestrator_details:
   156→            if not orchestrator_task_id_ref:
   157→                raise ValueError(f"Travel segment {task_id} missing orchestrator_details and orchestrator_task_id_ref")
   158→            orchestrator_task_raw_params_json = db_ops.get_task_params(orchestrator_task_id_ref)
   159→            if orchestrator_task_raw_params_json:
   160→                fetched_params = json.loads(orchestrator_task_raw_params_json) if isinstance(orchestrator_task_raw_params_json, str) else orchestrator_task_raw_params_json
   161→                orchestrator_details = fetched_params.get("orchestrator_details")
   162→
   163→        if not orchestrator_details:
   164→            raise ValueError(f"Travel segment {task_id}: Could not retrieve orchestrator_details")
   165→
   166→    # individual_segment_params has highest priority for segment-specific overrides
   167→    individual_params: IndividualSegmentParams = segment_params.get("individual_segment_params", {})
   168→
   169→    return SegmentContext(
   170→        mode="standalone" if is_standalone else "orchestrator",
   171→        orchestrator_details=orchestrator_details,
   172→        individual_params=individual_params,
   173→        segment_idx=segment_idx,
   174→        segment_params=segment_params,
   175→        orchestrator_task_id_ref=orchestrator_task_id_ref,
   176→        orchestrator_run_id=orchestrator_run_id,
   177→    )
   178→
   179→
   180→def _resolve_generation_inputs(ctx: SegmentContext, task_id: str, main_output_dir_base: Path) -> GenerationInputs:
   181→    """Resolve model, prompt, resolution, frame count, output directory, and flags.
   182→
   183→    Returns a GenerationInputs dataclass with all resolved values.
   184→    Raises ValueError if resolution format is invalid.
   185→    """
   186→    segment_params = ctx.segment_params
   187→    orchestrator_details = ctx.orchestrator_details
   188→    individual_params = ctx.individual_params
   189→    segment_idx = ctx.segment_idx
   190→
   191→    # Model name: segment > orchestrator (required)
   192→    model_name = segment_params.get("model_name") or orchestrator_details.get("model_name")
   193→    if not model_name:
   194→        raise ValueError(f"Travel segment {task_id}: model_name missing from both segment_params and orchestrator_details")
   195→
   196→    # Prompts: enhanced_prompt preferred > base_prompt fallback
   197→    # Frontend may provide both: enhanced_prompt (AI-enhanced) and base_prompt (user's original)
   198→    enhanced_prompt = _get_param("enhanced_prompt", individual_params, segment_params, default=None)
   199→    base_prompt = _get_param("base_prompt", individual_params, segment_params, default=" ")
   200→
   201→    # Use enhanced_prompt if present and non-empty, otherwise base_prompt
   202→    effective_prompt = enhanced_prompt if enhanced_prompt and enhanced_prompt.strip() else base_prompt
   203→    prompt_for_wgp = ensure_valid_prompt(effective_prompt)
   204→
   205→    if enhanced_prompt and enhanced_prompt.strip():
   206→        task_logger.debug(f"[PROMPT] Task {task_id}: Using enhanced_prompt")
   207→    negative_prompt_for_wgp = ensure_valid_negative_prompt(
   208→        _get_param("negative_prompt", individual_params, segment_params, default=" ")
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    # Resolution: segment > orchestrator
   212→    parsed_res_wh_str = segment_params.get("parsed_resolution_wh") or orchestrator_details.get("parsed_resolution_wh")
   213→    if not parsed_res_wh_str:
   214→        raise ValueError(f"Travel segment {task_id}: parsed_resolution_wh missing from both segment_params and orchestrator_details")
   215→    parsed_res_raw = parse_resolution(parsed_res_wh_str)
   216→    if parsed_res_raw is None:
   217→        raise ValueError(f"Travel segment {task_id}: Invalid resolution format {parsed_res_wh_str}")
   218→    from source.utils.resolution_utils import get_model_grid_size
   219→    model_name = segment_params.get("model_name") or orchestrator_details.get("model_name")
   220→    grid_size = get_model_grid_size(model_name)
   221→    parsed_res_wh = snap_resolution_to_model_grid(parsed_res_raw, grid_size)
   222→
   223→    # Frame count: individual_segment_params.num_frames > top-level num_frames > segment_frames_target > segment_frames_expanded[idx]
   224→    total_frames_for_segment = (
   225→        individual_params.get("num_frames") or
   226→        segment_params.get("num_frames") or
   227→        segment_params.get("segment_frames_target")
   228→    )
   229→    if not total_frames_for_segment:
   230→        segment_frames = orchestrator_details.get("segment_frames_expanded", [])
   231→        if segment_idx < len(segment_frames):
   232→            total_frames_for_segment = segment_frames[segment_idx]
   233→        else:
   234→            raise ValueError(
   235→                f"Travel segment {task_id}: no frame count found (segment_idx={segment_idx}, "
   236→                f"segment_frames_expanded has {len(segment_frames)} entries)"
   237→            )
   238→
   239→    current_run_base_output_dir_str = _get_param(
   240→        "current_run_base_output_dir",
   241→        segment_params,
   242→        default=orchestrator_details.get("main_output_dir_for_run", str(main_output_dir_base.resolve())),
   243→        prefer_truthy=True)
   244→
   245→    # Convert to Path and resolve relative paths against main_output_dir_base
   246→    base_dir_path = Path(current_run_base_output_dir_str)
   247→    if not base_dir_path.is_absolute():
   248→        # Relative path - resolve against main_output_dir_base
   249→        current_run_base_output_dir = main_output_dir_base / base_dir_path
   250→    else:
   251→        # Already absolute - use as is
   252→        current_run_base_output_dir = base_dir_path
   253→
   254→    segment_processing_dir = current_run_base_output_dir
   255→    segment_processing_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   256→
   257→    debug_enabled = _get_param("debug_mode_enabled", segment_params, orchestrator_details, default=False)
   258→    travel_mode = orchestrator_details.get("model_type", "vace")
   259→
   260→    return GenerationInputs(
   261→        model_name=model_name,
   262→        prompt_for_wgp=prompt_for_wgp,
   263→        negative_prompt_for_wgp=negative_prompt_for_wgp,
   264→        parsed_res_wh=parsed_res_wh,
   265→        total_frames_for_segment=total_frames_for_segment,
   266→        current_run_base_output_dir=current_run_base_output_dir,
   267→        segment_processing_dir=segment_processing_dir,
   268→        debug_enabled=debug_enabled,
   269→        travel_mode=travel_mode,
   270→    )
   271→
   272→
   273→def _resolve_image_references(ctx: SegmentContext, gen: GenerationInputs, task_id: str, is_standalone: bool) -> ImageRefs:
   274→    """Resolve start/end image reference paths and SVI predecessor video.
   275→
   276→    Handles SVI chaining (downloading predecessor video, extracting last frames),
   277→    non-SVI image resolution from multiple sources, and downloading URLs to local paths.
   278→    """
   279→    segment_params = ctx.segment_params
   280→    orchestrator_details = ctx.orchestrator_details
   281→    individual_params = ctx.individual_params
   282→    segment_idx = ctx.segment_idx
   283→    segment_processing_dir = gen.segment_processing_dir
   284→    debug_enabled = gen.debug_enabled
   285→
   286→    start_ref_path = None
   287→    end_ref_path = None
   288→
   289→    # Image resolution priority:
   290→    # 1. individual_segment_params.start_image_url / end_image_url
   291→    # 2. individual_segment_params.input_image_paths_resolved (array)
   292→    # 3. top-level input_image_paths_resolved
   293→    # 4. orchestrator_details images (indexed by segment_idx)
   294→
   295→    individual_images = individual_params.get("input_image_paths_resolved", [])
   296→    top_level_images = segment_params.get("input_image_paths_resolved", [])
   297→    orchestrator_images = orchestrator_details.get("input_image_paths_resolved", [])
   298→
   299→    task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: segment_idx={segment_idx}, individual_images={len(individual_images)}, top_level_images={len(top_level_images)}, orchestrator_images={len(orchestrator_images)}")
   300→
   301→    is_continuing = orchestrator_details.get("continue_from_video_resolved_path") is not None
   302→    # Check use_svi with explicit False handling (False at segment level should override True at orchestrator)
   303→    use_svi = segment_params["use_svi"] if "use_svi" in segment_params else orchestrator_details.get("use_svi", False)
   304→    svi_predecessor_video_url = _get_param(
   305→        "svi_predecessor_video_url", segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True
   306→    )
   307→
   308→    if use_svi:
   309→        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_MODE] Task {task_id}: SVI mode enabled for segment {segment_idx}")
   310→
   311→    # =============================================================================
   312→    # SVI MODE: Chain segments using predecessor video for overlapped_latents
   313→    # SVI Pro uses the last ~9 frames (5 + sliding_window_overlap) from predecessor
   314→    # to create temporal continuity via overlapped_latents in the VAE
   315→    # =============================================================================
   316→    svi_predecessor_video_for_source = None  # Will be set for SVI continuation
   317→
   318→    if use_svi and (segment_idx > 0 or svi_predecessor_video_url):
   319→        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Using SVI end frame chaining mode")
   320→
   321→        predecessor_output_url = None
   322→
   323→        # Priority 1: Manually specified predecessor video URL
   324→        if svi_predecessor_video_url:
   325→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Using manually specified predecessor video: {svi_predecessor_video_url}")
   326→            predecessor_output_url = svi_predecessor_video_url
   327→        # Priority 2: Fetch from dependency chain (for segment_idx > 0)
   328→        elif segment_idx > 0:
   329→            task_dependency_id, predecessor_output_url = db_ops.get_predecessor_output_via_edge_function(task_id)
   330→            if task_dependency_id and predecessor_output_url:
   331→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Found predecessor {task_dependency_id} with output: {predecessor_output_url}")
   332→            else:
   333→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: ERROR - Could not fetch predecessor output (dep_id={task_dependency_id})")
   334→
   335→        if predecessor_output_url:
   336→            # Download predecessor video if it's a URL
   337→            predecessor_video_path = predecessor_output_url
   338→            if predecessor_output_url.startswith("http"):
   339→                try:
   340→                    from source.utils import download_file as download_file
   341→                    local_filename = Path(predecessor_output_url).name
   342→                    local_download_path = segment_processing_dir / f"svi_predecessor_{segment_idx:02d}_{local_filename}"
   343→
   344→                    if not local_download_path.exists():
   345→                        download_file(predecessor_output_url, segment_processing_dir, local_download_path.name)
   346→                        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Downloaded predecessor video to {local_download_path}")
   347→                    else:
   348→                        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Predecessor video already exists at {local_download_path}")
   349→
   350→                    predecessor_video_path = str(local_download_path)
   351→                except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_dl:
   352→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Failed to download predecessor video: {e_dl}")
   353→                    predecessor_video_path = None
   354→
   355→            # SVI CRITICAL: Extract only the last ~9 frames (5 + overlap_size) from predecessor
   356→            # WGP uses prefix_video[:, -(5 + overlap_size):] to create overlapped_latents,
   357→            # but then prepends the ENTIRE prefix_video to output. By extracting only the
   358→            # last frames ourselves, we limit what gets prepended.
   359→            if predecessor_video_path and Path(predecessor_video_path).exists():
   360→                from source.media.video import (
   361→                    get_video_frame_count_and_fps as get_video_frame_count_and_fps,
   362→                    extract_frame_range_to_video as extract_frame_range_to_video
   363→                )
   364→
   365→                # Get predecessor video frame count
   366→                pred_frames, pred_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(predecessor_video_path)
   367→                if pred_frames and pred_frames > 0:
   368→                    # IMPORTANT GROUND TRUTH:
   369→                    # Wan2GP SVI continuation uses the last (5 + overlap_size) frames of prefix_video
   370→                    # to build `overlapped_latents`. The extra 5 frames are *model context*; the
   371→                    # overlap_size (4) is the actual stitch overlap we keep in the final segment.
   372→                    #
   373→                    # So we intentionally extract MORE than 4 frames here (usually 9), but after WGP
   374→                    # generation we trim the output so only the last 4 prefix frames remain as the
   375→                    # overlap with the previous segment.
   376→                    prefix_min_context = 5
   377→                    overlap_size = 4  # SVI_STITCH_OVERLAP (pixel-frame overlap)
   378→                    frames_needed = prefix_min_context + overlap_size  # 9 frames total
   379→                    start_frame = max(0, int(pred_frames) - frames_needed)
   380→
   381→                    # GROUND TRUTH LOG: Predecessor video analysis
   382→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: ========== PREDECESSOR ANALYSIS ==========")
   383→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: Predecessor video: {predecessor_video_path}")
   384→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: Predecessor total frames: {pred_frames}")
   385→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: prefix_min_context={prefix_min_context}, overlap_size={overlap_size} => frames_needed={frames_needed}")
   386→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: Extracting frames [{start_frame}:{pred_frames}] (last {frames_needed} frames)")
   387→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: Frame range breakdown:")
   388→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}:   - Frames 0-{start_frame-1}: Will be DISCARDED (not needed)")
   389→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}:   - Frames {start_frame}-{pred_frames-1}: Will be EXTRACTED (last {frames_needed} frames)")
   390→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}:   - Last 4 frames [{pred_frames-4}:{pred_frames-1}]: OVERLAP frames for stitching")
   391→
   392→                    trimmed_prefix_filename = f"svi_prefix_{segment_idx:02d}_last{frames_needed}frames_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}.mp4"
   393→                    trimmed_prefix_path = segment_processing_dir / trimmed_prefix_filename
   394→
   395→                    try:
   396→                        trimmed_result = extract_frame_range_to_video(
   397→                            input_video_path=predecessor_video_path,
   398→                            output_video_path=str(trimmed_prefix_path),
   399→                            start_frame=start_frame,
   400→                            end_frame=None,  # To end
   401→                            fps=float(pred_fps) if pred_fps and pred_fps > 0 else 16.0)
   402→                        # Verify extracted prefix video
   403→                        prefix_frames, prefix_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(trimmed_result)
   404→                        svi_predecessor_video_for_source = str(trimmed_result)
   405→                        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: Extracted prefix video: {prefix_frames} frames (expected: {frames_needed})")
   406→                        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: Prefix video path: {trimmed_result}")
   407→                        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: This prefix will be passed to WGP as video_source")
   408→                        if prefix_frames != frames_needed:
   409→                            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Seg {segment_idx}: WARNING: Extracted {prefix_frames} frames, expected {frames_needed}")
   410→                    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_trim:
   411→                        # Fallback: use full video if extraction failed
   412→                        svi_predecessor_video_for_source = predecessor_video_path
   413→                        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: WARNING - Failed to extract last frames ({e_trim}), using full predecessor video")
   414→                else:
   415→                    # Fallback: use full video if we can't get frame count
   416→                    svi_predecessor_video_for_source = predecessor_video_path
   417→                    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Could not get predecessor frame count, using full video")
   418→
   419→                # Still extract last frame for image_refs (anchor reference)
   420→                start_ref_path = extract_last_frame_as_image(
   421→                    predecessor_video_path,
   422→                    segment_processing_dir,
   423→                    task_id
   424→                )
   425→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: Extracted last frame as anchor start_ref: {start_ref_path}")
   426→            else:
   427→                task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: ERROR - Predecessor video not available at {predecessor_video_path}")
   428→
   429→        # For SVI, end_ref is the target image from input array
   430→        target_end_idx = segment_idx + 1 if segment_idx > 0 else 1
   431→        if svi_predecessor_video_url and segment_idx == 0:
   432→            target_end_idx = 1 if len(orchestrator_images) > 1 else 0
   433→
   434→        if len(orchestrator_images) > target_end_idx:
   435→            end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[target_end_idx]
   436→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: end_ref from input_images[{target_end_idx}]: {end_ref_path}")
   437→        elif len(orchestrator_images) > 0:
   438→            end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[-1]
   439→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: end_ref fallback to last input image: {end_ref_path}")
   440→
   441→    # =============================================================================
   442→    # SVI MODE: First segment uses input images normally (no manual predecessor)
   443→    # =============================================================================
   444→    elif use_svi and segment_idx == 0:
   445→        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_CHAINING] Seg {segment_idx}: First segment in SVI mode - using input images")
   446→        if len(orchestrator_images) > 0:
   447→            start_ref_path = orchestrator_images[0]
   448→        if len(orchestrator_images) > 1:
   449→            end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[1]
   450→
   451→    # =============================================================================
   452→    # NON-SVI MODES: Original logic
   453→    # =============================================================================
   454→    # Check individual_segment_params first (highest priority for standalone)
   455→    elif individual_params.get("start_image_url") or individual_params.get("end_image_url"):
   456→        start_ref_path = individual_params.get("start_image_url")
   457→        end_ref_path = individual_params.get("end_image_url")
   458→        task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: Using individual_segment_params URLs: start={start_ref_path}")
   459→    elif len(individual_images) >= 2:
   460→        start_ref_path = individual_images[0]
   461→        end_ref_path = individual_images[1]
   462→        task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: Using individual_segment_params array: start={start_ref_path}")
   463→    elif is_standalone and len(top_level_images) >= 2:
   464→        start_ref_path = top_level_images[0]
   465→        end_ref_path = top_level_images[1]
   466→        task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: Using top-level images directly: start={start_ref_path}")
   467→    elif is_continuing:
   468→        if segment_idx == 0:
   469→            continued_video_path = orchestrator_details.get("continue_from_video_resolved_path")
   470→            if continued_video_path and Path(continued_video_path).exists():
   471→                start_ref_path = extract_last_frame_as_image(continued_video_path, segment_processing_dir, task_id)
   472→            if orchestrator_images:
   473→                end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[0]
   474→        else:
   475→            if len(orchestrator_images) > segment_idx:
   476→                start_ref_path = orchestrator_images[segment_idx - 1]
   477→                end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[segment_idx]
   478→    else:
   479→        task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: Using orchestrator images (from scratch)")
   480→        if len(orchestrator_images) > segment_idx:
   481→            start_ref_path = orchestrator_images[segment_idx]
   482→
   483→        if len(orchestrator_images) > segment_idx + 1:
   484→            end_ref_path = orchestrator_images[segment_idx + 1]
   485→    task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: start_ref_path after logic: {start_ref_path}")
   486→
   487→    if start_ref_path:
   488→        start_ref_path = download_image_if_url(start_ref_path, segment_processing_dir, task_id, debug_mode=debug_enabled)
   489→        task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: start_ref_path AFTER DOWNLOAD: {start_ref_path}")
   490→    if end_ref_path:
   491→        end_ref_path = download_image_if_url(end_ref_path, segment_processing_dir, task_id, debug_mode=debug_enabled)
   492→
   493→    return ImageRefs(
   494→        start_ref_path=start_ref_path,
   495→        end_ref_path=end_ref_path,
   496→        svi_predecessor_video_for_source=svi_predecessor_video_for_source,
   497→        use_svi=use_svi,
   498→        is_continuing=is_continuing,
   499→    )
   500→
   501→
   502→def _process_structure_guidance(ctx: SegmentContext, gen: GenerationInputs, task_id: str, main_output_dir_base: Path, mask_active_frames: bool) -> StructureOutputs:
   503→    """Run TravelSegmentProcessor for VACE mode or any mode with structure guidance.
   504→
   505→    Returns StructureOutputs with guide/mask video paths and prompt type.
   506→    Raises RuntimeError/ValueError/OSError if the processor fails.
   507→    """
   508→    segment_params = ctx.segment_params
   509→    orchestrator_details = ctx.orchestrator_details
   510→    segment_idx = ctx.segment_idx
   511→    segment_processing_dir = gen.segment_processing_dir
   512→    travel_mode = gen.travel_mode
   513→    debug_enabled = gen.debug_enabled
   514→
   515→    guide_video_path = None
   516→    mask_video_path_for_wgp = None
   517→    video_prompt_type_str = None
   518→
   519→    # Always parse unified structure guidance config up-front so later logic can rely on it.
   520→    # This handles both new format (structure_guidance.videos) and legacy params (structure_videos).
   521→    structure_config = StructureGuidanceConfig.from_params({
   522→        **orchestrator_details,
   523→        **segment_params
   524→    })
   525→
   526→    # Check if structure guidance is configured (uses unified config)
   527→    has_structure_guidance = structure_config.has_guidance
   528→
   529→    # Run TravelSegmentProcessor for:
   530→    # 1. VACE mode (always - requires masks and guides)
   531→    # 2. Any mode with structure guidance configured (enables uni3c/flow guidance for i2v, etc.)
   532→    if travel_mode == "vace" or has_structure_guidance:
   533→        if has_structure_guidance and travel_mode != "vace":
   534→            task_logger.debug(f"[STRUCTURE_GUIDANCE] Task {task_id}: Running TravelSegmentProcessor for {travel_mode} mode (structure_guidance configured)")
   535→        try:
   536→            processor_context = TravelSegmentContext(
   537→                task_id=task_id,
   538→                segment_idx=segment_idx,
   539→                model_name=gen.model_name,
   540→                total_frames_for_segment=gen.total_frames_for_segment,
   541→                parsed_res_wh=gen.parsed_res_wh,
   542→                segment_processing_dir=segment_processing_dir,
   543→                main_output_dir_base=main_output_dir_base,
   544→                orchestrator_details=orchestrator_details,
   545→                segment_params=segment_params,
   546→                mask_active_frames=mask_active_frames,
   547→                debug_enabled=debug_enabled)
   548→            processor = TravelSegmentProcessor(processor_context)
   549→            segment_outputs = processor.process_segment()
   550→
   551→            guide_video_path = segment_outputs.get("video_guide")
   552→            mask_video_path_for_wgp = Path(segment_outputs["video_mask"]) if segment_outputs.get("video_mask") else None
   553→            video_prompt_type_str = segment_outputs["video_prompt_type"]
   554→            detected_structure_type = segment_outputs.get("structure_type")
   555→
   556→            # Debug: Log segment_outputs keys and structure_type
   557→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_DEBUG] Task {task_id}: segment_outputs keys: {list(segment_outputs.keys())}")
   558→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_DEBUG] Task {task_id}: detected_structure_type={repr(detected_structure_type)}, guide_video_path={bool(guide_video_path)}")
   559→
   560→            # If config targets uni3c and we have a guide video, update the config's guidance URL
   561→            # This is used later by the UNI3C MODE section
   562→            if structure_config.is_uni3c and guide_video_path:
   563→                task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_AUTO] Task {task_id}: Uni3C mode with guide from processor: {guide_video_path}")
   564→                # Store the processor's guide video path in the config
   565→                structure_config._guidance_video_url = guide_video_path
   566→
   567→        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e_shared_processor:
   568→            task_logger.error(f"Shared processor failed: {e_shared_processor}", exc_info=True)
   569→            raise RuntimeError(f"Shared processor failed: {e_shared_processor}") from e_shared_processor
   570→
   571→    return StructureOutputs(
   572→        guide_video_path=guide_video_path,
   573→        mask_video_path_for_wgp=mask_video_path_for_wgp,
   574→        video_prompt_type_str=video_prompt_type_str,
   575→        structure_config=structure_config,
   576→    )
   577→
   578→
   579→def _build_generation_params(ctx: SegmentContext, gen: GenerationInputs, image_refs: ImageRefs, structure: StructureOutputs, task_id: str) -> dict:
   580→    """Build the WGP generation_params dict from resolved inputs.
   581→
   582→    Handles base params, image paths, additional_loras, explicit overrides
   583→    (num_inference_steps, guidance_scale, flow_shift), per-segment LoRAs,
   584→    phase_config parsing, and guide/mask video attachment.
   585→    """
   586→    segment_params = ctx.segment_params
   587→    orchestrator_details = ctx.orchestrator_details
   588→    individual_params = ctx.individual_params
   589→    debug_enabled = gen.debug_enabled
   590→    model_name = gen.model_name
   591→    start_ref_path = image_refs.start_ref_path
   592→    end_ref_path = image_refs.end_ref_path
   593→    guide_video_path = structure.guide_video_path
   594→    mask_video_path_for_wgp = structure.mask_video_path_for_wgp
   595→    video_prompt_type_str = structure.video_prompt_type_str
   596→    structure_config = structure.structure_config
   597→
   598→    # Seed: individual_segment_params > top-level > default
   599→    seed_to_use = individual_params.get("seed_to_use") or segment_params.get("seed_to_use", 12345)
   600→
   601→    generation_params = {
   602→        "model_name": model_name,
   603→        "negative_prompt": gen.negative_prompt_for_wgp,
   604→        "resolution": f"{gen.parsed_res_wh[0]}x{gen.parsed_res_wh[1]}",
   605→        "video_length": gen.total_frames_for_segment,
   606→        "seed": seed_to_use,
   607→    }
   608→
   609→    # Always pass images if available, regardless of specific travel_mode string (hybrid models need them)
   610→    if start_ref_path:
   611→        generation_params["image_start"] = str(Path(start_ref_path).resolve())
   612→    if end_ref_path:
   613→        generation_params["image_end"] = str(Path(end_ref_path).resolve())
   614→
   615→    task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: generation_params image_start: {generation_params.get('image_start')}")
   616→    task_logger.debug(f"[IMG_RESOLVE] Task {task_id}: generation_params image_end: {generation_params.get('image_end')}")
   617→
   618→    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   619→    # Parameter extraction with precedence: individual > segment > orchestrator
   620→    # Using _get_param() helper for consistent fallback behavior
   621→    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   622→
   623→    # Travel-only: pass `additional_loras` through on the segment task payload if present,
   624→    # but DO NOT propagate into `generation_params` (so it won't trigger LoRA downloads/application).
   625→    additional_loras = _get_param(
   626→        "additional_loras",
   627→        individual_params,
   628→        segment_params,
   629→        orchestrator_details,
   630→        default={},
   631→        prefer_truthy=True,  # match previous `a or b or c` behavior for empty dicts
   632→    )
   633→    if additional_loras and debug_enabled:
   634→        task_logger.debug(
   635→            f"[TRAVEL_LORA] Task {task_id}: additional_loras present on payload but intentionally ignored for generation ({len(additional_loras)} entries)"
   636→        )
   637→
   638→    # IMPORTANT: Do NOT treat orchestrator/UI `steps` as diffusion `num_inference_steps`.
   639→    # In travel payloads, `steps` often means "timeline steps" (UI concept), whereas
   640→    # diffusion steps must come from `num_inference_steps` (or `phase_config.steps_per_phase`).
   641→    explicit_steps = _get_param("num_inference_steps", segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True)
   642→    if explicit_steps:
   643→        generation_params["num_inference_steps"] = explicit_steps
   644→
   645→    explicit_guidance = _get_param("guidance_scale", segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True)
   646→    if explicit_guidance:
   647→        generation_params["guidance_scale"] = explicit_guidance
   648→
   649→    explicit_flow_shift = _get_param("flow_shift", segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True)
   650→    if explicit_flow_shift:
   651→        generation_params["flow_shift"] = explicit_flow_shift
   652→
   653→    # =============================================================================
   654→    # Per-Segment Parameter Overrides
   655→    # =============================================================================
   656→    # Log what per-segment overrides were received
   657→    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: ========== Per-Segment Override Check ==========")
   658→    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: individual_params keys: {list(individual_params.keys()) if individual_params else 'None'}")
   659→
   660→    # Check for segment-specific LoRAs first (highest priority)
   661→    # This allows per-segment LoRA overrides to take precedence over phase_config LoRAs
   662→    segment_loras = _get_param("segment_loras", individual_params, default=None)
   663→    segment_lora_config = None
   664→
   665→    if segment_loras:
   666→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_LORAS] Task {task_id}: Using segment-specific LoRAs ({len(segment_loras)} LoRAs)")
   667→        # Pass through URLs/filenames without downloading - convert_to_wgp_task_impl() handles resolution
   668→        segment_lora_config = True  # Flag to skip phase_config LoRAs below
   669→        activated = []
   670→        mults = []
   671→        for lora_dict in segment_loras:
   672→            path = lora_dict.get("path", "")
   673→            strength = lora_dict.get("strength", 1.0)
   674→            if path:
   675→                activated.append(path)
   676→                mults.append(str(strength))
   677→        if activated:
   678→            generation_params["activated_loras"] = activated
   679→            generation_params["loras_multipliers"] = " ".join(mults)
   680→            headless_logger.info(f"Set {len(activated)} segment-specific LoRAs (download deferred)", task_id=task_id)
   681→    else:
   682→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_LORAS] Task {task_id}: No segment-specific LoRAs - will use phase_config/shot-level LoRAs if present")
   683→
   684→    # Determine phase_config source for logging
   685→    phase_config_from_individual = individual_params.get("phase_config") if individual_params else None
   686→    phase_config_from_segment = segment_params.get("phase_config") if segment_params else None
   687→    _phase_config_from_orchestrator = orchestrator_details.get("phase_config") if orchestrator_details else None
   688→
   689→    phase_config_source = _get_param(
   690→        "phase_config", individual_params, segment_params, orchestrator_details, prefer_truthy=True
   691→    )
   692→
   693→    # Log which source phase_config came from
   694→    if phase_config_source:
   695→        if phase_config_from_individual:
   696→            source_name = "PER-SEGMENT (individual_params)"
   697→        elif phase_config_from_segment:
   698→            source_name = "segment_params"
   699→        else:
   700→            source_name = "SHOT-LEVEL (orchestrator_details)"
   701→
   702→        preset_name = phase_config_source.get("preset_name", phase_config_source.get("name", "unknown"))
   703→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: Using phase_config from {source_name}")
   704→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: preset_name={preset_name}")
   705→
   706→    if phase_config_source:
   707→        try:
   708→            steps_per_phase = phase_config_source.get("steps_per_phase", [2, 2, 2])
   709→            phase_config_steps = sum(steps_per_phase)
   710→            task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: steps_per_phase={steps_per_phase} (total={phase_config_steps})")
   711→
   712→            parsed_phase_config = parse_phase_config(
   713→                phase_config=phase_config_source,
   714→                num_inference_steps=phase_config_steps,
   715→                task_id=task_id,
   716→                model_name=generation_params.get("model_name"),
   717→                debug_mode=debug_enabled
   718→            )
   719→
   720→            generation_params["num_inference_steps"] = phase_config_steps
   721→
   722→            # Copy non-LoRA params from phase_config
   723→            for key in ["guidance_phases", "switch_threshold", "switch_threshold2",
   724→                       "guidance_scale", "guidance2_scale", "guidance3_scale",
   725→                       "flow_shift", "sample_solver", "model_switch_phase"]:
   726→                if key in parsed_phase_config and parsed_phase_config[key] is not None:
   727→                    generation_params[key] = parsed_phase_config[key]
   728→
   729→            # Copy LoRA params from phase_config ONLY if no segment-specific LoRAs
   730→            # (segment_loras takes precedence over phase_config LoRAs)
   731→            # Pass through URLs without downloading - convert_to_wgp_task_impl() handles resolution
   732→            if not segment_lora_config:
   733→                if "lora_names" in parsed_phase_config:
   734→                    generation_params["activated_loras"] = parsed_phase_config["lora_names"]
   735→                if "lora_multipliers" in parsed_phase_config:
   736→                    generation_params["loras_multipliers"] = " ".join(str(m) for m in parsed_phase_config["lora_multipliers"])
   737→                lora_count = len(parsed_phase_config.get("lora_names", []))
   738→                if lora_count:
   739→                    headless_logger.info(f"Set {lora_count} phase_config LoRAs (download deferred)", task_id=task_id)
   740→            else:
   741→                task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: Skipping phase_config LoRAs (segment-specific LoRAs take precedence)")
   742→
   743→            # Pass phase_config patch data through to task_processor.py for proper apply+restore lifecycle
   744→            # (instead of applying directly here where it would never be cleaned up)
   745→            if "_patch_config" in parsed_phase_config:
   746→                generation_params["_parsed_phase_config"] = parsed_phase_config
   747→                generation_params["_phase_config_model_name"] = model_name
   748→
   749→        except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
   750→            raise ValueError(f"Task {task_id}: Invalid phase_config: {e}") from e
   751→    else:
   752→        task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_PHASE_CONFIG] Task {task_id}: No phase_config found - using explicit params only")
   753→
   754→    # Summary log for per-segment overrides
   755→    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: ========== Per-Segment Override Summary ==========")
   756→    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: LoRAs applied: {generation_params.get('activated_loras', [])}")
   757→    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: LoRA multipliers: {generation_params.get('loras_multipliers', '')}")
   758→    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: num_inference_steps: {generation_params.get('num_inference_steps', 'not set')}")
   759→    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: guidance_scale: {generation_params.get('guidance_scale', 'not set')}")
   760→    task_logger.debug(f"[PER_SEGMENT_HANDLER] Task {task_id}: ================================================")
   761→
   762→    if guide_video_path: generation_params["video_guide"] = str(guide_video_path)
   763→    if mask_video_path_for_wgp: generation_params["video_mask"] = str(mask_video_path_for_wgp.resolve())
   764→    generation_params["video_prompt_type"] = video_prompt_type_str
   765→
   766→    # Diagnostic: Log guidance configuration when uni3c is enabled
   767→    if structure_config.is_uni3c and guide_video_path:
   768→        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK] Task {task_id}: Checking guidance configuration...")
   769→        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   video_guide path: {guide_video_path}")
   770→        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   structure_config.guidance_video_url: {structure_config.guidance_video_url}")
   771→        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   image_end (i2v): {generation_params.get('image_end', 'None')}")
   772→        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   video_prompt_type: '{video_prompt_type_str}'")
   773→        if "V" in video_prompt_type_str:
   774→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   🔴 WARNING: video_prompt_type has 'V' - VACE will ALSO use video_guide!")
   775→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   This causes double-feeding: both VACE and Uni3C use same guide")
   776→        else:
   777→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   ✅ OK: video_prompt_type='{video_prompt_type_str}' (no 'V')")
   778→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_GUIDANCE_CHECK]   Uni3C handles motion guidance, VACE only uses mask for frame preservation")
   779→
   780→    return generation_params
   781→
   782→
   783→def _apply_svi_config(generation_params: dict, ctx: SegmentContext, gen: GenerationInputs, image_refs: ImageRefs, task_id: str) -> None:
   784→    """Apply SVI-specific generation parameters (mutates generation_params in place).
   785→
   786→    Enables SVI encoding mode, sets video_prompt_type, configures predecessor video
   787→    as video_source, adjusts video_length for continuation frame accounting,
   788→    copies SVI generation params, and merges SVI LoRAs.
   789→    """
   790→    if not image_refs.use_svi:
   791→        return
   792→
   793→    segment_params = ctx.segment_params
   794→    orchestrator_details = ctx.orchestrator_details
   795→    start_ref_path = image_refs.start_ref_path
   796→    svi_predecessor_video_for_source = image_refs.svi_predecessor_video_for_source
   797→    total_frames_for_segment = gen.total_frames_for_segment
   798→
   799→    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Configuring WGP payload for SVI mode")
   800→
   801→    # Enable SVI encoding mode
   802→    generation_params["svi2pro"] = True
   803→
   804→    # SVI requires video_prompt_type="I" to enable image_refs passthrough
   805→    generation_params["video_prompt_type"] = "I"
   806→
   807→    # Set image_refs to start image (anchor for SVI encoding)
   808→    if start_ref_path:
   809→        generation_params["image_refs_paths"] = [str(Path(start_ref_path).resolve())]
   810→        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set image_refs_paths to start image: {start_ref_path}")
   811→
   812→    # CRITICAL: Pass predecessor video as video_source for SVI continuation
   813→    # WGP uses prefix_video[:, -(5 + overlap_size):] to create overlapped_latents
   814→    # This provides temporal continuity between segments (uses last ~9 frames)
   815→    # IMPORTANT: Must NOT set image_start when video_source is set, otherwise
   816→    # wgp.py prioritizes image_start and creates only a 1-frame prefix_video!
   817→    if svi_predecessor_video_for_source:
   818→        from source.media.video import get_video_frame_count_and_fps as get_video_frame_count_and_fps
   819→
   820→        video_source_path = str(Path(svi_predecessor_video_for_source).resolve())
   821→        generation_params["video_source"] = video_source_path
   822→
   823→        # GROUND TRUTH LOG: What WGP will receive
   824→        try:
   825→            wgp_input_frames, wgp_input_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(video_source_path)
   826→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: ========== WGP INPUT ANALYSIS ==========")
   827→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: video_source: {video_source_path}")
   828→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: video_source frames: {wgp_input_frames}")
   829→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: WGP will extract last 9 frames from this for overlapped_latents")
   830→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: WGP will prepend ALL {wgp_input_frames} frames to output")
   831→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_wgp_log:
   832→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_GROUND_TRUTH] Task {task_id}: Could not analyze video_source: {e_wgp_log}")
   833→
   834→        # Remove image_start so WGP uses video_source for multi-frame prefix_video
   835→        # The anchor image is provided via image_refs instead
   836→        if "image_start" in generation_params:
   837→            del generation_params["image_start"]
   838→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Removed image_start (anchor provided via image_refs)")
   839→        # CRITICAL: Set image_prompt_type to include "V" to enable video_source usage
   840→        # SVI2Pro allows "SVL" - we use "SV" for start+video continuation
   841→        generation_params["image_prompt_type"] = "SV"
   842→        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set image_prompt_type='SV' for video continuation")
   843→        task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set video_source for SVI continuation: {svi_predecessor_video_for_source}")
   844→
   845→    # SVI Pro sliding window overlap = 4 frames (standard for SVI)
   846→    # Note: WGP applies latent alignment formula (x-1)//4*4+1, but we bypass it
   847→    # by patching sliding_window_defaults.overlap_default=4 in headless_model_management.py
   848→    generation_params["sliding_window_overlap"] = 4
   849→    task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set sliding_window_overlap=4 for SVI")
   850→
   851→    # IMPORTANT (SVI continuation frame accounting):
   852→    # When `video_source` is provided, WGP will:
   853→    #   - generate `video_length` frames total
   854→    #   - discard the first `sliding_window_overlap` frames of the generated sample
   855→    #   - prepend the prefix video frames (pixels) to the output
   856→    #
   857→    # Therefore the number of **new** frames produced by diffusion is:
   858→    #   new_frames = video_length - sliding_window_overlap
   859→    #
   860→    # In our travel pipeline, `segment_frames_target` / `segment_frames_expanded[idx]` represent
   861→    # the desired number of **new** frames for the segment (4N+1). To actually get that many
   862→    # new frames while still maintaining overlap for stitching, we must request:
   863→    #   video_length = desired_new_frames + sliding_window_overlap
   864→    if svi_predecessor_video_for_source:
   865→        try:
   866→            desired_new_frames = int(total_frames_for_segment) if total_frames_for_segment else 0
   867→            overlap_size = int(generation_params.get("sliding_window_overlap") or 4)
   868→            current_video_length = int(generation_params.get("video_length") or 0)
   869→            if desired_new_frames > 0 and overlap_size > 0:
   870→                # Only bump if the payload is still "new-frames based" (avoid double-bumping).
   871→                if current_video_length == desired_new_frames:
   872→                    generation_params["video_length"] = desired_new_frames + overlap_size
   873→                    task_logger.debug(
   874→                        f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: SVI continuation => bump video_length "
   875→                        f"{current_video_length} -> {generation_params['video_length']} "
   876→                        f"(desired_new_frames={desired_new_frames}, overlap_size={overlap_size})"
   877→                    )
   878→                else:
   879→                    task_logger.debug(
   880→                        f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: SVI continuation => NOT bumping video_length "
   881→                        f"(current={current_video_length}, desired_new_frames={desired_new_frames}, overlap_size={overlap_size})"
   882→                    )
   883→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as e_len_bump:
   884→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: WARNING: Could not adjust video_length for SVI continuation: {e_len_bump}")
   885→
   886→    # Add SVI generation parameters from segment_params (set by orchestrator)
   887→    for key in ["guidance_phases", "num_inference_steps", "guidance_scale", "guidance2_scale",
   888→               "flow_shift", "switch_threshold", "model_switch_phase", "sample_solver"]:
   889→        if key in segment_params and segment_params[key] is not None:
   890→            generation_params[key] = segment_params[key]
   891→            task_logger.debug(f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Set {key}={segment_params[key]}")
   892→
   893→    # Merge SVI LoRAs with existing LoRAs
   894→    from source.task_handlers.travel.svi_config import merge_svi_into_generation_params
   895→
   896→    svi_strength = _get_param("svi_strength", segment_params, orchestrator_details)
   897→    svi_strength_1 = _get_param("svi_strength_1", segment_params, orchestrator_details)
   898→    svi_strength_2 = _get_param("svi_strength_2", segment_params, orchestrator_details)
   899→
   900→    merge_svi_into_generation_params(
   901→        generation_params,
   902→        svi_strength=svi_strength,
   903→        svi_strength_1=svi_strength_1,
   904→        svi_strength_2=svi_strength_2)
   905→
   906→    task_logger.debug(
   907→        f"[SVI_PAYLOAD] Task {task_id}: Merged SVI LoRAs "
   908→        f"(svi_strength={svi_strength}, svi_strength_1={svi_strength_1}, svi_strength_2={svi_strength_2})"
   909→    )
   910→
   911→
   912→def _apply_uni3c_config(generation_params: dict, ctx: SegmentContext, gen: GenerationInputs, structure: StructureOutputs, task_id: str) -> None:
   913→    """Apply UNI3C-specific generation parameters (mutates generation_params in place).
   914→
   915→    Downloads the guide video if it is a URL, then injects all uni3c_* parameters
   916→    into generation_params.
   917→    """
   918→    structure_config = structure.structure_config
   919→    segment_params = ctx.segment_params
   920→    segment_processing_dir = gen.segment_processing_dir
   921→
   922→    # Use unified structure_config exclusively
   923→    use_uni3c = structure_config.is_uni3c
   924→
   925→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C_DEBUG] Task {task_id}: structure_config.is_uni3c={structure_config.is_uni3c}, use_uni3c={use_uni3c}")
   926→
   927→    if not use_uni3c:
   928→        return
   929→
   930→    from source.utils import download_file as download_file
   931→
   932→    # Get guide video from config (may have been set by processor)
   933→    uni3c_guide = structure_config.guidance_video_url
   934→
   935→    # If Uni3C is enabled but there's no guide video, skip to avoid downstream crashes.
   936→    if not uni3c_guide:
   937→        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Uni3C requested but no guide video provided; skipping Uni3C injection")
   938→        return
   939→
   940→    # Use config values (already handles legacy param fallback)
   941→    uni3c_strength = structure_config.strength
   942→    uni3c_start = structure_config.step_window[0]
   943→    uni3c_end = structure_config.step_window[1]
   944→    uni3c_keep_gpu = structure_config.keep_on_gpu
   945→    uni3c_frame_policy = structure_config.frame_policy
   946→    uni3c_zero_empty = structure_config.zero_empty_frames
   947→
   948→    # Download guide video if URL
   949→    if uni3c_guide.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
   950→        local_filename = Path(uni3c_guide).name or "uni3c_guide_video.mp4"
   951→        local_download_path = segment_processing_dir / f"uni3c_{local_filename}"
   952→        if not local_download_path.exists():
   953→            download_file(uni3c_guide, segment_processing_dir, local_download_path.name)
   954→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Downloaded guide video to {local_download_path}")
   955→        else:
   956→            task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Guide video already exists at {local_download_path}")
   957→        uni3c_guide = str(local_download_path)
   958→
   959→    # Layer 2 logging
   960→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Uni3C ENABLED (via structure_guidance config)")
   961→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   guide_video={uni3c_guide}")
   962→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   strength={uni3c_strength}")
   963→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   start_percent={uni3c_start}")
   964→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   end_percent={uni3c_end}")
   965→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   frame_policy={uni3c_frame_policy}")
   966→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   keep_on_gpu={uni3c_keep_gpu}")
   967→    task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}:   zero_empty_frames={uni3c_zero_empty}")
   968→
   969→    # Inject into generation_params
   970→    generation_params["use_uni3c"] = True
   971→    generation_params["uni3c_guide_video"] = uni3c_guide
   972→    generation_params["uni3c_strength"] = uni3c_strength
   973→    generation_params["uni3c_start_percent"] = uni3c_start
   974→    generation_params["uni3c_end_percent"] = uni3c_end
   975→    generation_params["uni3c_keep_on_gpu"] = uni3c_keep_gpu
   976→    generation_params["uni3c_frame_policy"] = uni3c_frame_policy
   977→    generation_params["uni3c_zero_empty_frames"] = uni3c_zero_empty
   978→
   979→    # For i2v with end anchor: blackout last uni3c frame so i2v handles it alone
   980→    # This prevents uni3c from "overcooking" the final frame
   981→    is_last_segment = segment_params.get("is_last_segment", True)
   982→    has_end_anchor = generation_params.get("image_end") is not None
   983→    if is_last_segment and has_end_anchor:
   984→        generation_params["uni3c_blackout_last_frame"] = True
   985→        task_logger.debug(f"[UNI3C] Task {task_id}: Will blackout last frame (is_last_segment + has end anchor)")
   986→
   987→
   988→def handle_travel_segment_via_queue(task_params_dict: dict, main_output_dir_base: Path, task_id: str, colour_match_videos: bool, mask_active_frames: bool, task_queue: HeadlessTaskQueue, is_standalone: bool = False):
   989→    """Handle travel segment tasks via direct queue integration to eliminate blocking waits.
   990→
   991→    Orchestrator mode (is_standalone=False) requires orchestrator_task_id_ref, orchestrator_run_id, segment_index.
   992→    Standalone mode (is_standalone=True) uses orchestrator_details provided directly in params.
   993→    Parameter precedence: individual_segment_params > segment_params > orchestrator_details.
   994→    """
   995→    headless_logger.debug(f"Starting travel segment queue processing (standalone={is_standalone})", task_id=task_id)
   996→    log_ram_usage("Segment via queue - start", task_id=task_id)
   997→
   998→    try:
   999→        from source.task_handlers.travel.chaining import handle_travel_chaining_after_wgp
  1000→
  1001→        # 1. Resolve segment context (mode, orchestrator_details, individual_params, segment_idx)
  1002→        ctx = _resolve_segment_context(task_params_dict, is_standalone, task_id)
  1003→
  1004→        # 2. Resolve generation inputs (model, prompt, resolution, frames, output dir, flags)
  1005→        gen = _resolve_generation_inputs(ctx, task_id, main_output_dir_base)
  1006→
  1007→        # 3. Resolve image references (SVI chaining, non-SVI image resolution, downloading)
  1008→        image_refs = _resolve_image_references(ctx, gen, task_id, is_standalone)
  1009→
  1010→        # 4. Process structure guidance (TravelSegmentProcessor)
  1011→        structure = _process_structure_guidance(ctx, gen, task_id, main_output_dir_base, mask_active_frames)
  1012→
  1013→        # 5. Build generation_params dict
  1014→        generation_params = _build_generation_params(ctx, gen, image_refs, structure, task_id)
  1015→
  1016→        # 6. Apply SVI-specific configuration
  1017→        _apply_svi_config(generation_params, ctx, gen, image_refs, task_id)
  1018→        # 7. Apply UNI3C-specific configuration
  1019→        _apply_uni3c_config(generation_params, ctx, gen, structure, task_id)
  1020→
  1021→        # Log diagnostic summary before WGP submission
  1022→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: ========== WGP GENERATION REQUEST ==========")
  1023→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: video_length (target frames): {generation_params.get('video_length')}")
  1024→        is_valid_4n1 = (generation_params.get('video_length', 0) - 1) % 4 == 0
  1025→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: Valid 4N+1: {is_valid_4n1} {'✓' if is_valid_4n1 else '✗ WARNING'}")
  1026→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: video_guide: {generation_params.get('video_guide', 'None')}")
  1027→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: video_mask: {generation_params.get('video_mask', 'None')}")
  1028→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: model: {gen.model_name}")
  1029→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: resolution: {generation_params.get('resolution')}")
  1030→        task_logger.debug(f"[WGP_SUBMIT] Task {task_id}: =============================================")
  1031→
  1032→        # IMPORTANT: Use the DB task_id as the queue task id.
  1033→        # This keeps logs, fatal error handling, and debug tooling consistent (no "travel_seg_" indirection).
  1034→        # We still include a hint in parameters so the queue can apply any task-type specific behavior.
  1035→        generation_params["_source_task_type"] = "travel_segment"
  1036→        from headless_model_management import GenerationTask
  1037→
  1038→        generation_task = GenerationTask(
  1039→            id=task_id,
  1040→            model=gen.model_name,
  1041→            prompt=gen.prompt_for_wgp,
  1042→            parameters=generation_params
  1043→        )
  1044→
  1045→        _submitted_task_id = task_queue.submit_task(generation_task)
  1046→
  1047→        max_wait_time = 1800
  1048→        wait_interval = 2
  1049→        elapsed_time = 0
  1050→
  1051→        while elapsed_time < max_wait_time:
  1052→            status = task_queue.get_task_status(task_id)
  1053→            if status is None: return False, f"Travel segment {task_id}: Task status became None"
  1054→
  1055→            if status.status == "completed":
  1056→                # In standalone mode, skip chaining (no orchestrator to coordinate with)
  1057→                if is_standalone:
  1058→                    headless_logger.debug(f"Standalone segment completed, skipping chaining", task_id=task_id)
  1059→                    return True, status.result_path
  1060→
  1061→                # Orchestrator mode: run chaining
  1062→                chain_success, chain_message, final_chained_path = handle_travel_chaining_after_wgp(
  1063→                    wgp_task_params={
  1064→                        # Provide minimal ground-truth frame params for downstream debug logging / trimming analysis.
  1065→                        "use_svi": image_refs.use_svi,
  1066→                        "video_length": generation_params.get("video_length"),
  1067→                        "sliding_window_overlap": generation_params.get("sliding_window_overlap"),
  1068→                        "video_source": generation_params.get("video_source"),
  1069→                        "travel_chain_details": {
  1070→                            "orchestrator_task_id_ref": ctx.orchestrator_task_id_ref,
  1071→                            "orchestrator_run_id": ctx.orchestrator_run_id,
  1072→                            "segment_index_completed": ctx.segment_idx,
  1073→                            "orchestrator_details": ctx.orchestrator_details,
  1074→                            "segment_processing_dir_for_saturation": str(gen.segment_processing_dir),
  1075→                            "is_first_new_segment_after_continue": ctx.segment_params.get("is_first_segment", False) and ctx.orchestrator_details.get("continue_from_video_resolved_path"),
  1076→                            "is_subsequent_segment": not ctx.segment_params.get("is_first_segment", True),
  1077→                            "colour_match_videos": colour_match_videos,
  1078→                            "cm_start_ref_path": None, "cm_end_ref_path": None, "show_input_images": False, "start_image_path": None, "end_image_path": None,
  1079→                        }
  1080→                    },
  1081→                    actual_wgp_output_video_path=status.result_path,
  1082→                    image_download_dir=gen.segment_processing_dir,
  1083→                    main_output_dir_base=main_output_dir_base)
  1084→                if chain_success and final_chained_path:
  1085→                    return True, final_chained_path
  1086→                else:
  1087→                    return True, status.result_path
  1088→            elif status.status == "failed":
  1089→                return False, f"Travel segment {task_id}: Generation failed: {status.error_message}"
  1090→
  1091→            time.sleep(wait_interval)
  1092→            elapsed_time += wait_interval
  1093→
  1094→        return False, f"Travel segment {task_id}: Generation timeout"
  1095→
  1096→    except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError, KeyError) as e:
  1097→        task_logger.error(f"Travel segment {task_id}: Exception: {e}", exc_info=True)
  1098→        return False, f"Travel segment {task_id}: Exception: {str(e)}"
  1099→
  1100→class TaskRegistry:
  1101→    """Registry for task handlers."""

  1103→    @staticmethod

  1105→        """Dispatch a task to the appropriate handler.
  1106→

  1110→                     main_output_dir_base, project_id, task_queue, etc.
  1111→

  1114→        """
  1115→        task_id = context["task_id"]
  1116→        params = context["task_params_dict"]

  1120→            return TaskRegistry._handle_direct_queue_task(task_type, context)
  1121→

  1160→            "edit_video_orchestrator": lambda: handle_edit_video_orchestrator_task(
  1161→                task_params_from_db=params,

  1164→                orchestrator_project_id=context["project_id"]),
  1165→            "join_clips_segment": lambda: handle_join_clips_task(
  1166→                task_params_from_db=params,
  1167→                main_output_dir_base=context["main_output_dir_base"],

  1194→
  1195→        if task_type in handlers:
  1196→            # Orchestrator setup
  1197→            if task_type in ["travel_orchestrator", "join_clips_orchestrator", "edit_video_orchestrator"]:
  1198→                params["task_id"] = task_id

  1210→    @staticmethod
  1211→    def _handle_direct_queue_task(task_type: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:
  1212→        task_id = context["task_id"]
  1213→        task_queue = context["task_queue"]
  1214→        params = context["task_params_dict"]
  1215→        
  1216→        try:
  1217→            generation_task = db_task_to_generation_task(
  1218→                params, task_id, task_type, context["wan2gp_path"], context["debug_mode"]
  1219→            )
  1220→            
  1221→            if task_type == "wan_2_2_t2i":
  1222→                generation_task.parameters["video_length"] = 1
  1223→            
  1224→            if context["colour_match_videos"]:
  1225→                generation_task.parameters["colour_match_videos"] = True
  1226→            if context["mask_active_frames"]:
  1227→                generation_task.parameters["mask_active_frames"] = True
  1228→            
  1229→            task_queue.submit_task(generation_task)
  1230→            
  1231→            # Wait for completion
  1232→            max_wait_time = 3600
  1233→            elapsed = 0
  1234→            while elapsed < max_wait_time:
  1235→                status = task_queue.get_task_status(task_id)
  1236→                if not status: return False, "Task status became None"
  1237→                
  1238→                if status.status == "completed":
  1239→                    return True, status.result_path
  1240→                elif status.status == "failed":
  1241→                    return False, status.error_message or "Failed without message"
  1242→                
  1243→                time.sleep(2)
  1244→                elapsed += 2
  1245→            
  1246→            return False, "Timeout"
  1247→            
  1248→        except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
  1249→            task_logger.error(f"Queue error: {e}", exc_info=True)
  1250→            return False, f"Queue error: {e}"
  1251→

1→"""
     2→Task processing, generation execution, and worker/monitor loops.
     3→
     4→Contains the core logic for processing a single generation task
     5→(``process_task_impl``), delegating the actual generation to
     6→headless_wgp.py via the orchestrator (``execute_generation_impl``),
     7→and the ``worker_loop`` / ``_monitor_loop`` entry-points used by
     8→``queue_lifecycle``.
     9→
    10→Every public function takes the ``HeadlessTaskQueue`` instance
    11→(aliased *queue*) as its first argument.
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from __future__ import annotations
    15→
    16→import logging
    17→import os
    18→import queue as queue_mod
    19→import threading
    20→import time
    21→import traceback
    22→from pathlib import Path
    23→from typing import Any
    24→
    25→# Re-export so callers that previously reached cleanup_memory_after_task
    26→# via worker_thread (and now via task_processor) continue to work.
    27→from source.task_handlers.queue.memory_cleanup import cleanup_memory_after_task  # noqa: F401
    28→
    29→from source.core.log import queue_logger
    30→
    31→
    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→# Thread safety for WGP global monkey-patching
    34→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    35→_wgp_patch_lock = threading.Lock()
    36→
    37→
    38→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    39→# Task processing
    40→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→
    42→def process_task_impl(queue: Any, task: Any, worker_name: str):
    43→    """
    44→    Process a single generation task.
    45→
    46→    This is where we delegate to headless_wgp.py while managing
    47→    model persistence and state.
    48→    """
    49→    # Ensure logs emitted during this generation are attributed to this task.
    50→    # This runs inside the GenerationWorker thread, which is where wgp/headless_wgp runs.
    51→    try:
    52→        from source.core.log import set_current_task_context  # local import to avoid cycles
    53→        set_current_task_context(task.id)
    54→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError) as e:
    55→        # Swallowing is intentional: logging context is optional and failures are debug-only
    56→        logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue').debug(f"Failed to set task logging context for {task.id}: {e}")
    57→
    58→    with queue.queue_lock:
    59→        queue.current_task = task
    60→        task.status = "processing"
    61→
    62→    queue.logger.info(f"{worker_name} processing task {task.id}")
    63→    start_time = time.time()
    64→
    65→    try:
    66→        # 1. Ensure correct model is loaded (orchestrator checks WGP's ground truth)
    67→        queue._switch_model(task.model, worker_name)
    68→
    69→        # 2. Reset billing start time now that model is loaded
    70→        # This ensures users aren't charged for model loading time
    71→        try:
    72→            from source.db_operations import reset_generation_started_at
    73→            reset_generation_started_at(task.id)
    74→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_billing:
    75→            # Don't fail the task if billing reset fails - just log it
    76→            queue.logger.warning(f"[BILLING] Failed to reset generation_started_at for task {task.id}: {e_billing}")
    77→
    78→        # 3. Delegate actual generation to orchestrator
    79→        # The orchestrator handles the heavy lifting while we manage the queue
    80→        result_path = queue._execute_generation(task, worker_name)
    81→
    82→        # Verify we're still in Wan2GP directory after generation
    83→        current_dir = os.getcwd()
    84→        if "Wan2GP" not in current_dir:
    85→            queue.logger.warning(
    86→                f"[PATH_CHECK] After generation: Current directory changed!\n"
    87→                f"  Current: {current_dir}\n"
    88→                f"  Expected: Should contain 'Wan2GP'\n"
    89→                f"  This may cause issues for subsequent tasks!"
    90→            )
    91→        else:
    92→            queue.logger.debug(f"[PATH_CHECK] After generation: Still in Wan2GP")
    93→
    94→        # 3. Validate output and update task status
    95→        processing_time = time.time() - start_time
    96→        is_success = bool(result_path)
    97→        try:
    98→            if is_success:
    99→                # If a path was returned, check existence where possible
   100→                rp = Path(result_path)
